Prior bug:\n  # `grep -oE 'Track [0-9]+' | head -1` on whole file returned archive-prose\n  # Track ref (e.g. \"Track 5\" from S53 narrative) instead of currently-active\n  # Track. Per L-S56-1 3-class typology: target is Class B free-form-prose-in-\n  # markdown, so `^` anchor advice from Check 8 was INVALID (Track refs don't\n  # line-anchor in this file format). Real fix is structural — parse the\n  # \"## Active Focus Track\" section bounded by next `---` separator FIRST via\n  # awk range pattern, then grep section-content only. Archive prose lives\n  # OUTSIDE the section so head -1 picks active Track ref, or empty if Phase\n  # 3.5 uses T<N> shorthand exclusively (also correct: ACTIVE_TRACK=\"\" → no\n  # spurious match in queued-grill awk per `track != \"\"` guard line ~98).\n  # `|| true` end-of-pipeline preserved (L-S48d-1 pipefail+ERR-trap discipline).\n  ACTIVE_TRACK=$(awk '/^## Active Focus Track/,/^---/' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null | grep -oE 'Track [0-9]+' | head -1 || true)\n  GRILL_QUEUE_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.queued-grill-fired.log\"\n  # Match entries whose fire_when contains active session \"S<N>\" or track \"Track <N>\" tokens.\n  awk -v phase=\"$ACTIVE_PHASE\" -v sess=\"$ACTIVE_SESSION\" -v track=\"$ACTIVE_TRACK\" '\n    /^### Q-/ { qid=$2; in_entry=1; fire_when=\"\"; next }\n    in_entry && /^- \\*\\*fire_when\\*\\*:/ {\n      sub(/^- \\*\\*fire_when\\*\\*: */,\"\"); fire_when=$0;\n      match_hit=0;\n      if (sess  != \"\" && index(fire_when, sess)  > 0) match_hit=1\n      if (track != \"\" && index(fire_when, track) > 0) match_hit=1\n      if (match_hit) print \"[queued-grill] \" qid \" fire_when=\" fire_when\n    }\n    /^### / && !/^### Q-/ { in_entry=0 }\n  ' \"$QUEUED_GRILL_FILE\" >> \"$GRILL_QUEUE_LOG\" 2>/dev/null || true\nfi\n\n# Build resume context.\nCONTEXT=\"STOCKFORGE AUTONOMOUS RESUME: SessionStart hook fired. \\\nRead these in order before any tool call: \\\n(1) $LATEST [handoff checkpoint with current phase/track/next_action], \\\n(2) $PROJECT_DIR/CLAUDE.md [project rules: NO LLM math, every claim has source+as-of, deterministic risk, multi-perspective adversarial], \\\n(3) $PROJECT_DIR/PROJECT_CHARTER.md [immutable invariants I-S1..I-S35], \\\n(4) $PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md [routing + active phase/track], \\\n(5) $PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations/queued-grill-master.md [check fire_when triggers matching active context], \\\n(6) Active session plan via current-execution.md routing table. \\\nThen resume at checkpoint's next_action. Do NOT commit (CLAUDE.md hard rule). For Agent dispatches: run_in_background=true.\"\n\nnode -e \"\nconst ctx = process.argv[1];\nprocess.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({\n  hookSpecificOutput: {\n    hookEventName: 'SessionStart',\n    additionalContext: ctx\n  }\n}));\n\" \"$CONTEXT\"\n\necho \"[$(date -Iseconds)] SessionStart-bootstrap injected additionalContext\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n\nprintf 'ready_at=%s\\nsession_id=%s\\nsource=%s\\n' \"$(date -Iseconds)\" \"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\" \"$SOURCE\" \\\n  > \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-ready\"\n\n# Spawn DETACHED continue-injector that SendKeys \"continue\" to fresh TUI.\n# GATING (S8 revision per user \"no continue, why? fix\" complaint, supersedes S7 unconditional gate):\n#   source=clear  → fire UNCONDITIONALLY (explicit user /clear → expects checkpoint resume; race risk low)\n#   source=resume → fire UNCONDITIONALLY (user invoked claude --resume → expects continuation)\n#   source=startup → gated by autonomous_mode=true (fresh launch; user likely typing own prompt → race risk high)\n# stale-prompt-detector.sh line 26 short-circuits literal \"continue\" prompt → no false stale-warning firing.\nEXEC_FILE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/current-execution.md\"\nAUTONOMOUS_MODE=\"false\"\nif [ -f \"$EXEC_FILE\" ]; then\n  AUTONOMOUS_MODE=$(awk -F': ' '/^\\*\\*autonomous_mode\\*\\*/ {print $2; exit}' \"$EXEC_FILE\" 2>/dev/null \\\n    | awk '{print $1}' | tr -d '*' || echo \"false\")\n  AUTONOMOUS_MODE=\"${AUTONOMOUS_MODE:-false}\"\nfi\n\nSHOULD_FIRE_INJECTOR=\"false\"\nFIRE_REASON=\"\"\ncase \"$SOURCE\" in\n  clear)\n    # HH-H.3 (S48m): gate clear-branch by autonomous_mode + STOCKFORGE_FORCE_CONTINUE_ON_CLEAR\n    # override. Pre-S48m unconditionally fired — but a user-driven /clear with\n    # autonomous_mode=false (post-Phase-2.5 ratification gate) would race against\n    # the human typing their own first prompt. Override env var keeps the explicit-\n    # user-flow path fast.\n    if [ \"${STOCKFORGE_FORCE_CONTINUE_ON_CLEAR:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n      SHOULD_FIRE_INJECTOR=\"true\"\n      FIRE_REASON=\"source=clear + STOCKFORGE_FORCE_CONTINUE_ON_CLEAR=1 (override)\"\n    elif [ \"$AUTONOMOUS_MODE\" = \"true\" ]; then\n      SHOULD_FIRE_INJECTOR=\"true\"\n      FIRE_REASON=\"source=clear + autonomous_mode=true\"\n    else\n      FIRE_REASON=\"source=clear + autonomous_mode=false (HH-H.3 gate; user-driven /clear → race risk)\"\n    fi\n    ;;\n  resume)\n    SHOULD_FIRE_INJECTOR=\"true\"\n    FIRE_REASON=\"source=resume (user invoked claude --resume)\"\n    ;;\n  startup)\n    if [ \"$AUTONOMOUS_MODE\" = \"true\" ]; then\n      SHOULD_FIRE_INJECTOR=\"true\"\n      FIRE_REASON=\"source=startup + autonomous_mode=true\"\n    else\n      FIRE_REASON=\"source=startup + autonomous_mode=false (race risk with user typing)\"\n    fi\n    ;;\nesac\n\nif [ \"$SHOULD_FIRE_INJECTOR\" = \"true\" ]; then\n  case \"$(uname -s)\" in\n    MINGW*|MSYS*|CYGWIN*)\n      PS_SCRIPT=\"$(cygpath -w \"$PROJECT_DIR/scripts/hooks/continue-injector.ps1\")\"\n      powershell.exe -NoProfile -WindowStyle Hidden -ExecutionPolicy Bypass \\\n        -Command \"Start-Process -WindowStyle Hidden -FilePath powershell.exe -ArgumentList '-NoProfile','-ExecutionPolicy','Bypass','-File','$PS_SCRIPT'\" \\\n        >/dev/null 2>&1 &\n      disown 2>/dev/null || true\n      ;;\n    Darwin)\n      nohup osascript -e 'delay 2.5' \\\n        -e 'tell application \"System Events\" to keystroke \"continue\"' \\\n        -e 'tell application \"System Events\" to key code 36' \\\n        >/dev/null 2>&1 &\n      disown 2>/dev/null || true\n      ;;\n    Linux)\n      if command -v xdotool >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n        ( sleep 2.5; xdotool type --delay 30 \"continue\"; xdotool key Return ) \\\n          >/dev/null 2>&1 &\n        disown 2>/dev/null || true\n      fi\n      ;;\n  esac\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds)] SessionStart-bootstrap spawned continue-injector ($FIRE_REASON)\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nelse\n  echo \"[$(date -Iseconds)] SessionStart-bootstrap SKIPPED continue-injector ($FIRE_REASON)\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","numLines":204,"startLine":1,"totalLines":204}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"70bbc67c-1165-4470-ad55-2133a21ec027","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"c9bd3831-b7df-41f7-be20-c5e77bdf7468","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

Surfaces detected\n# unattested files via notification + .unattested-observations.tsv registry. Does NOT\n# auto-fire D1 logic — agent decides whether to manually attest.\n#\n# Wired: SessionStart (after session-start-bootstrap.sh, in startup|resume|clear matcher block)\n# Kill switch: STOCKFORGE_UNATTESTED_SCAN_DISABLE=1\n# Per L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware: if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y\n# Per L-S68-2 find pipefail guard: glob expansion guarded with [ -f ]\n# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nOBS_DIR=\"$MEMORY_DIR/observations\"\nATTEST_LOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/attestation-log.tsv\"\nREGISTRY=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.unattested-observations.tsv\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n\n# Kill switch\nif [ \"${STOCKFORGE_UNATTESTED_SCAN_DISABLE:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Read SessionStart payload (best-effort)\nPAYLOAD=\"$(cat 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nSOURCE=\"\"\nif [ -n \"$PAYLOAD\" ]; then\n  SOURCE=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"source\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"source\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\nfi\ncase \"$SOURCE\" in\n  startup|resume|clear|\"\") ;;\n  *) exit 0 ;;\nesac\n\n# No observations dir → nothing to scan\nif [ ! -d \"$OBS_DIR\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\nTS=\"$(date -u +%FT%TZ 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n\n# Initialise registry header if missing\nif [ ! -f \"$REGISTRY\" ]; then\n  mkdir -p \"$(dirname \"$REGISTRY\")\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  printf 'detected_ts\\tsession_id\\tobservation_basename\\tmarker_present\\tlog_row_present\\n' > \"$REGISTRY\"\nfi\n\nUNATTESTED=\"\"\nUNATTESTED_COUNT=0\n\n# D1 attestation contract scope = sandwich-dev-*.md only\nfor f in \"$OBS_DIR\"/sandwich-dev-*.md; do\n  if [ ! -f \"$f\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  bn=\"$(basename \"$f\" .md)\"\n  marker=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.attestation-checked-${bn}\"\n  marker_present=\"0\"\n  log_row_present=\"0\"\n\n  if [ -f \"$marker\" ]; then\n    marker_present=\"1\"\n  fi\n\n  if [ -f \"$ATTEST_LOG\" ]; then\n    if grep -qF \"$bn\" \"$ATTEST_LOG\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      log_row_present=\"1\"\n    fi\n  fi\n\n  if [ \"$marker_present\" = \"0\" ] && [ \"$log_row_present\" = \"0\" ]; then\n    UNATTESTED_COUNT=$((UNATTESTED_COUNT + 1))\n    UNATTESTED=\"${UNATTESTED}${bn}\\n\"\n    if ! grep -qF \"\t${bn}\t\" \"$REGISTRY\" 2>/dev/null; then\n      printf '%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n' \\\n        \"$TS\" \"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\" \"$bn\" \"$marker_present\" \"$log_row_present\" >> \"$REGISTRY\"\n    fi\n  fi\ndone\n\nif [ \"$UNATTESTED_COUNT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  mkdir -p \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/${TS//[:.]/-}-unattested-observations.md\"\n  {\n    printf '# Unattested sandwich-dev observations (L-S68-1 mitigation)\\n\\n'\n    printf '**Detected at**: %s (SessionStart scan)\\n' \"$TS\"\n    printf '**Session**: %s\\n' \"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\n    printf '**Source**: %s\\n\\n' \"${SOURCE:-unknown}\"\n    printf '## Files lacking BOTH attestation marker AND attestation-log row\\n\\n'\n    printf '%b' \"$UNATTESTED\" | awk 'NF>0 {print \"- `\"$0\"`\"}'\n    printf '\\n## Recommended action\\n\\n'\n    printf 'Per L-S68-1: when /clear (or other event) killed the SubagentStop chain,\\n'\n    printf 'the post-dev-dispatch-attestation-check.sh hook never fired. Manual fallback:\\n\\n'\n    printf '1. Read each observation frontmatter; cross-check claimed metrics empirically\\n'\n    printf '   (pytest + git-status)\\n'\n    printf '2. If divergence → treat as M-S67-3 / M-S66-1 pattern; catalog in mistake-log\\n\\n'\n    printf 'Registry log: `agent-workspace/memory/.unattested-observations.tsv`\\n'\n  } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n  printf 'WARN: %d unattested sandwich-dev observation(s) detected — see %s\\n' \\\n    \"$UNATTESTED_COUNT\" \"$NOTIF_FILE\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5494,"cache_read_input_tokens":125195,"output_tokens":8335,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":5494,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":8335,"cache_read_input_tokens":125195,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5494,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":5494},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CakdmsxiPm5vkJ7siZE9w","type":"assistant","uuid":"722b031e-6bc4-4513-a2da-959bdfb223c3","timestamp":"2026-05-06T03:51:34.171Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"c9bd3831-b7df-41f7-be20-c5e77bdf7468","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

fixtures via printf/cat heredoc.\n# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../session-start-scan-unattested-observations.sh\"\nif [ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: hook not found at $HOOK\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nPASS=0\nFAIL=0\n\nTEMPDIR=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/proj\"\nOBS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nATTEST_LOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/attestation-log.tsv\"\nREGISTRY=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.unattested-observations.tsv\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n\nsetup_sandbox() {\n  rm -rf \"$PROJECT_DIR\"\n  mkdir -p \"$OBS_DIR\" \"$MEMORY_DIR\" \"$NOTIF_DIR\"\n}\n\nmake_obs() {\n  local basename=\"$1\"\n  cat > \"$OBS_DIR/${basename}.md\" <<EOF\n---\nobservation_id: ${basename}\nagent: sandwich-dev\nverdict: READY-FOR-MAIN-VERIFY\n---\nbody\nEOF\n}\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local payload=\"$1\"\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"test-sess-$$\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$payload\" 2>/dev/null\n}\n\nassert_pass() { PASS=$((PASS+1)); printf 'PASS TC%s: %s\\n' \"$2\" \"$1\"; }\nassert_fail() { FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); printf 'FAIL TC%s: %s\\n' \"$2\" \"$1\"; }\n\nPAYLOAD_STARTUP='{\"hook_event_name\":\"SessionStart\",\"source\":\"startup\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\"}'\nPAYLOAD_CLEAR='{\"hook_event_name\":\"SessionStart\",\"source\":\"clear\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\"}'\nPAYLOAD_RESUME='{\"hook_event_name\":\"SessionStart\",\"source\":\"resume\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\"}'\nPAYLOAD_OTHER='{\"hook_event_name\":\"SessionStart\",\"source\":\"compact\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\"}'\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC1 — single unattested obs → registry row + notification + WARN to stderr\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc1\"\nSTDERR=\"$(run_hook \"$PAYLOAD_STARTUP\" 2>&1 >/dev/null || true)\"\nREG_ROWS=\"$(grep -c \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc1\" \"$REGISTRY\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nREG_ROWS=\"${REG_ROWS:-0}\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"$(ls \"$NOTIF_DIR\"/*-unattested-observations.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l || true)\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"${NOTIF_COUNT:-0}\"\nWARN_HIT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$STDERR\" | grep -c 'WARN.*unattested' 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nWARN_HIT=\"${WARN_HIT:-0}\"\nif [ \"$REG_ROWS\" -eq 1 ] && [ \"$NOTIF_COUNT\" -eq 1 ] && [ \"$WARN_HIT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"single unattested → registry row + notification + WARN stderr\" \"1\"\nelse\n  assert_fail \"TC1: reg_rows=$REG_ROWS notif=$NOTIF_COUNT warn=$WARN_HIT (expected 1/1/≥1)\" \"1\"\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC2 — obs with marker present → NOT flagged (attested via marker)\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc2\"\ntouch \"$MEMORY_DIR/.attestation-checked-sandwich-dev-S99-tc2\"\nrun_hook \"$PAYLOAD_STARTUP\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nREG_ROWS=\"$(grep -c \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc2\" \"$REGISTRY\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nREG_ROWS=\"${REG_ROWS:-0}\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"$(ls \"$NOTIF_DIR\"/*-unattested-observations.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l || true)\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"${NOTIF_COUNT:-0}\"\nif [ \"$REG_ROWS\" -eq 0 ] && [ \"$NOTIF_COUNT\" -eq 0 ]; run_hook suppresses it)\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc1\"\nSTDERR=\"$(CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"test-sess-tc1\" \\\n  bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$PAYLOAD_STARTUP\" 2>&1 >/dev/null || true)\"\nREG_ROWS=\"$(grep -c \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc1\" \"$REGISTRY\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nREG_ROWS=\"${REG_ROWS:-0}\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"$(ls \"$NOTIF_DIR\"/*-unattested-observations.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l || true)\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"${NOTIF_COUNT:-0}\"\nWARN_HIT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$STDERR\" | grep -c 'WARN.*unattested' 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nWARN_HIT=\"${WARN_HIT:-0}\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# session-start-scan-unattested-observations-fire-test.sh — Plan 012 D1 firing test.\n# Tests externally-observable behavior per L-S59-1:\n#   - .unattested-observations.tsv registry row appended\n#   - notification file created on detection\n#   - kill switch honored\n#   - source filter (only startup|resume|clear|empty)\n#   - dedup: re-fire same obs basename → no duplicate registry row\n# Per L-S52-3 success-path: real-data smoke + ≥6 TCs (≥1 positive + ≥3 negative + kill-switch).\n# Per L-S62-1: no yes|head patterns; fixtures via printf/cat heredoc.\n# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../session-start-scan-unattested-observations.sh\"\nif [ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: hook not found at $HOOK\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nPASS=0\nFAIL=0\n\nTEMPDIR=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/proj\"\nOBS_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/observations\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nATTEST_LOG=\"$MEMORY_DIR/attestation-log.tsv\"\nREGISTRY=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.unattested-observations.tsv\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n\nsetup_sandbox() {\n  rm -rf \"$PROJECT_DIR\"\n  mkdir -p \"$OBS_DIR\" \"$MEMORY_DIR\" \"$NOTIF_DIR\"\n}\n\nmake_obs() {\n  local basename=\"$1\"\n  cat > \"$OBS_DIR/${basename}.md\" <<EOF\n---\nobservation_id: ${basename}\nagent: sandwich-dev\nverdict: READY-FOR-MAIN-VERIFY\n---\nbody\nEOF\n}\n\nrun_hook() {\n  local payload=\"$1\"\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" CLAUDE_SESSION_ID=\"test-sess-$$\" \\\n    bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$payload\" 2>/dev/null\n}\n\nassert_pass() { PASS=$((PASS+1)); printf 'PASS TC%s: %s\\n' \"$2\" \"$1\"; }\nassert_fail() { FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); printf 'FAIL TC%s: %s\\n' \"$2\" \"$1\"; }\n\nPAYLOAD_STARTUP='{\"hook_event_name\":\"SessionStart\",\"source\":\"startup\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\"}'\nPAYLOAD_CLEAR='{\"hook_event_name\":\"SessionStart\",\"source\":\"clear\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\"}'\nPAYLOAD_RESUME='{\"hook_event_name\":\"SessionStart\",\"source\":\"resume\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\"}'\nPAYLOAD_OTHER='{\"hook_event_name\":\"SessionStart\",\"source\":\"compact\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\"}'\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC1 — single unattested obs → registry row + notification + WARN to stderr\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc1\"\nSTDERR=\"$(run_hook \"$PAYLOAD_STARTUP\" 2>&1 >/dev/null || true)\"\nREG_ROWS=\"$(grep -c \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc1\" \"$REGISTRY\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nREG_ROWS=\"${REG_ROWS:-0}\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"$(ls \"$NOTIF_DIR\"/*-unattested-observations.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l || true)\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"${NOTIF_COUNT:-0}\"\nWARN_HIT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$STDERR\" | grep -c 'WARN.*unattested' 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nWARN_HIT=\"${WARN_HIT:-0}\"\nif [ \"$REG_ROWS\" -eq 1 ] && [ \"$NOTIF_COUNT\" -eq 1 ] && [ \"$WARN_HIT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"single unattested → registry row + notification + WARN stderr\" \"1\"\nelse\n  assert_fail \"TC1: reg_rows=$REG_ROWS notif=$NOTIF_COUNT warn=$WARN_HIT (expected 1/1/≥1)\" \"1\"\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC2 — obs with marker present → NOT flagged (attested via marker)\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc2\"\ntouch \"$MEMORY_DIR/.attestation-checked-sandwich-dev-S99-tc2\"\nrun_hook \"$PAYLOAD_STARTUP\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nREG_ROWS=\"$(grep -c \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc2\" \"$REGISTRY\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nREG_ROWS=\"${REG_ROWS:-0}\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"$(ls \"$NOTIF_DIR\"/*-unattested-observations.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l || true)\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"${NOTIF_COUNT:-0}\"\nif [ \"$REG_ROWS\" -eq 0 ] && [ \"$NOTIF_COUNT\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"obs with marker → not flagged (attested)\" \"2\"\nelse\n  assert_fail \"TC2: reg_rows=$REG_ROWS notif=$NOTIF_COUNT (expected 0/0)\" \"2\"\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC3 — obs with attestation-log row → NOT flagged (attested via log)\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc3\"\nprintf 'ts\\tdispatch_id\\tobservation_passed\\tempirical_passed\\tdivergence\\tverdict\\n' > \"$ATTEST_LOG\"\nprintf '2026-05-06T00:00:00Z\\tsandwich-dev-S99-tc3\\t10\\t10\\t0\\tPASS\\n' >> \"$ATTEST_LOG\"\nrun_hook \"$PAYLOAD_STARTUP\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nREG_ROWS=\"$(grep -c \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc3\" \"$REGISTRY\" 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nREG_ROWS=\"${REG_ROWS:-0}\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"$(ls \"$NOTIF_DIR\"/*-unattested-observations.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l || true)\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"${NOTIF_COUNT:-0}\"\nif [ \"$REG_ROWS\" -eq 0 ] && [ \"$NOTIF_COUNT\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"obs with attestation-log row → not flagged (attested)\" \"3\"\nelse\n  assert_fail \"TC3: reg_rows=$REG_ROWS notif=$NOTIF_COUNT (expected 0/0)\" \"3\"\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC4 — kill switch STOCKFORGE_UNATTESTED_SCAN_DISABLE=1 → no scan, no registry, no notif\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc4\"\nSTOCKFORGE_UNATTESTED_SCAN_DISABLE=1 run_hook \"$PAYLOAD_STARTUP\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nREG_EXISTS=\"0\"\nif [ -f \"$REGISTRY\" ]; then REG_EXISTS=\"1\"; fi\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"$(ls \"$NOTIF_DIR\"/*-unattested-observations.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l || true)\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"${NOTIF_COUNT:-0}\"\nif [ \"$REG_EXISTS\" = \"0\" ] && [ \"$NOTIF_COUNT\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"kill switch → no scan, no registry, no notification\" \"4\"\nelse\n  assert_fail \"TC4: reg_exists=$REG_EXISTS notif=$NOTIF_COUNT (expected 0/0)\" \"4\"\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC5 — payload source=compact (not startup|resume|clear|empty) → no scan\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nmake_obs \"sandwich-dev-S99-tc5\"\nrun_hook \"$PAYLOAD_OTHER\" >/dev/null 2>&1\nREG_EXISTS=\"0\"\nif [ -f \"$REGISTRY\" ]; then REG_EXISTS=\"1\"; fi\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"$(ls \"$NOTIF_DIR\"/*-unattested-observations.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l || true)\"\nNOTIF_COUNT=\"${NOTIF_COUNT:-0}\"\nif [ \"$REG_EXISTS\" = \"0\" ] && [ \"$NOTIF_COUNT\" -eq 0 ]; {"parentUuid":"b88d0775-785b-4cf8-ba75-7fc1a0e9e8ff","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01G9b6D5iP95A5mEBHyhkqBB","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_017K3VXPko1ce3oHYshoaJ4t","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\pre-checkpoint-close-verifier.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# pre-checkpoint-close-verifier.sh — Stop hook detecting M-S67-3 pattern.\n# L-S67-5 mitigation: agent should run `git status --short` BEFORE authoring \"what\n# survived\" narrative in checkpoint close. M-S67-3 was: S67 checkpoint claimed only\n# D1 survived, but git status showed D2+D3+D5 also in working tree → narrative drift\n# from empirical state. This Stop hook detects post-hoc: if checkpoints/latest.md was\n# modified this session AND git status shows entries whose basenames are NOT mentioned\n# in the checkpoint text → emit WARN + notification (after whitelist filter).\n#\n# Wired: Stop (after autonomous-stop-watchdog.sh, before bash-hook-lint.sh)\n# Kill switch: STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_DISABLE=1\n# Detection signal: checkpoint mtime > .session-ready mtime (session-start-bootstrap.sh\n# writes .session-ready on every SessionStart per its line ~124).\n# Per L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware: if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y\n# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nCKPT=\"$MEMORY_DIR/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nSESSION_READY=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-ready\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n\n# Kill switch\nif [ \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_DISABLE:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Only fire on Stop event\nPAYLOAD=\"$(cat 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nHOOK_EVENT=\"\"\nif [ -n \"$PAYLOAD\" ]; then\n  HOOK_EVENT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"hook_event_name\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"hook_event_name\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\nfi\nif [ \"$HOOK_EVENT\" != \"Stop\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Skip if no checkpoint or no session marker\nif [ ! -f \"$CKPT\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$SESSION_READY\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Compare mtimes (Linux stat -c%Y / macOS stat -f%m fallback)\nCKPT_MTIME=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\nSESSION_START_TS=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n\n# If checkpoint not modified this session, skip\nif [ \"$CKPT_MTIME\" -le \"$SESSION_START_TS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Run git status --short in PROJECT_DIR\ncd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || exit 0\nGIT_STATUS=\"$(git status --short 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nif [ -z \"$GIT_STATUS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Whitelist: auto-generated/transient paths NOT expected in checkpoint narrative.\n# Tested against working-tree paths from `git status --short` output (after status-code prefix strip).\n# Configurable via env STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST (extra ERE patterns appended).\nWHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/sessions-rollup\\.tsv|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/)'\nif [ -n \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST:-}\" ]; then\n  WHITELIST_PATTERN=\"${WHITELIST_PATTERN}|${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST}\"\nfi\n\nUNMENTIONED=\"\"\nUNMENTIONED_COUNT=0\n\n# Iterate git status entries; skip whitelisted; check basename in checkpoint\nwhile IFS= read -r line; do\n  if [ -z \"$line\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  # `git status --short` output: 2-char status code + space + path (or rename has \" -> \")\n  # Strip first 3 chars to get path\n  path=\"${line:3}\"\n  # Handle rename: \"old -> new\" — take new path\n  if printf '%s' \"$path\" | grep -q ' -> '; then\n    path=\"$(printf '%s' \"$path\" | sed 's/.* -> //')\"\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"$path\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  # Whitelist check\n  if printf '%s' \"$path\" | grep -qE \"$WHITELIST_PATTERN\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  bn=\"$(basename \"$path\")\"\n  # Look for basename literal in checkpoint text\n  if ! grep -qF \"$bn\" \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    UNMENTIONED_COUNT=$((UNMENTIONED_COUNT + 1))\n    UNMENTIONED=\"${UNMENTIONED}${path}\\n\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$GIT_STATUS\"\n\nif [ \"$UNMENTIONED_COUNT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  TS=\"$(date -u +%FT%TZ 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  mkdir -p \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/${TS//[:.]/-}-checkpoint-mentions-incomplete.md\"\n  {\n    printf '# Checkpoint mentions incomplete (L-S67-5 / M-S67-3 prevention)\\n\\n'\n    printf '**Detected at**: %s (Stop hook)\\n' \"$TS\"\n    printf '**Checkpoint**: `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md`\\n'\n    printf '**Checkpoint mtime > session start**: %s > %s\\n\\n' \"$CKPT_MTIME\" \"$SESSION_START_TS\"\n    printf '## Git-status entries NOT mentioned by basename in checkpoint (after whitelist filter)\\n\\n'\n    printf '%b' \"$UNMENTIONED\" | awk 'NF>0 {print \"- `\"$0\"`\"}'\n    printf '\\n## Recommended action\\n\\n'\n    printf 'Per L-S67-5: agent must run `git status --short` BEFORE authoring checkpoint\\n'\n    printf '\"what survived\" narrative. The hook found entries above that exist in working tree\\n'\n    printf 'but do NOT appear by basename in the checkpoint text.\\n\\n'\n    printf '1. Re-read git status; cross-check intentional vs missed\\n'\n    printf '2. If intentional omission → ignore (false positive — consider extending whitelist via\\n'\n    printf '   `STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST` env var)\\n'\n    printf '3. If missed → amend checkpoint to reflect actual state (M-S67-3 recurrence pattern)\\n'\n  } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n  printf 'WARN: %d unmentioned git-status entry(ies) in checkpoint — see %s\\n' \\\n    \"$UNMENTIONED_COUNT\" \"$NOTIF_FILE\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":876,"cache_read_input_tokens":147035,"output_tokens":3947,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":876,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":3947,"cache_read_input_tokens":147035,"cache_creation_input_tokens":876,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":876},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011Cake1SDnxnUXb8UBUYb7m","type":"assistant","uuid":"33731d95-873b-4a10-92f1-71cea071e6ad","timestamp":"2026-05-06T03:53:59.058Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"c9bd3831-b7df-41f7-be20-c5e77bdf7468","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Only fire on Stop event\nPAYLOAD=\"$(cat 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nHOOK_EVENT=\"\"\nif [ -n \"$PAYLOAD\" ]; then\n  HOOK_EVENT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"hook_event_name\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"hook_event_name\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\nfi\nif [ \"$HOOK_EVENT\" != \"Stop\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Skip if no checkpoint or no session marker\nif [ ! -f \"$CKPT\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$SESSION_READY\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Compare mtimes (Linux stat -c%Y / macOS stat -f%m fallback)\nCKPT_MTIME=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\nSESSION_START_TS=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n\n# If checkpoint not modified this session, skip\nif [ \"$CKPT_MTIME\" -le \"$SESSION_START_TS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Run git status --short in PROJECT_DIR\ncd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || exit 0\nGIT_STATUS=\"$(git status --short 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nif [ -z \"$GIT_STATUS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Whitelist: auto-generated/transient paths NOT expected in checkpoint narrative.\n# Tested against working-tree paths from `git status --short` output (after status-code prefix strip).\n# Configurable via env STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST (extra ERE patterns appended).\nWHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/sessions-rollup\\.tsv|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/)'\nif [ -n \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST:-}\" ]; then\n  WHITELIST_PATTERN=\"${WHITELIST_PATTERN}|${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST}\"\nfi\n\nUNMENTIONED=\"\"\nUNMENTIONED_COUNT=0\n\n# Iterate git status entries; skip whitelisted; check basename in checkpoint\nwhile IFS= read -r line; do\n  if [ -z \"$line\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  # `git status --short` output: 2-char status code + space + path (or rename has \" -> \")\n  # Strip first 3 chars to get path\n  path=\"${line:3}\"\n  # Handle rename: \"old -> new\" — take new path\n  if printf '%s' \"$path\" | grep -q ' -> '; then\n    path=\"$(printf '%s' \"$path\" | sed 's/.* -> //')\"\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"$path\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  # Whitelist check\n  if printf '%s' \"$path\" | grep -qE \"$WHITELIST_PATTERN\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  bn=\"$(basename \"$path\")\"\n  # Look for basename literal in checkpoint text\n  if ! grep -qF \"$bn\" \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    UNMENTIONED_COUNT=$((UNMENTIONED_COUNT + 1))\n    UNMENTIONED=\"${UNMENTIONED}${path}\\n\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$GIT_STATUS\"\n\nif [ \"$UNMENTIONED_COUNT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  TS=\"$(date -u +%FT%TZ 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  mkdir -p \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/${TS//[:.]/-}-checkpoint-mentions-incomplete.md\"\n  {\n    printf '# Checkpoint mentions incomplete (L-S67-5 / M-S67-3 prevention)\\n\\n'\n    printf '**Detected at**: %s (Stop hook)\\n' \"$TS\"\n    printf '**Checkpoint**: `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md`\\n'\n    printf '**Checkpoint mtime > session start**: %s > %s\\n\\n' \"$CKPT_MTIME\" \"$SESSION_START_TS\"\n    printf '## Git-status entries NOT mentioned by basename in checkpoint (after whitelist filter)\\n\\n'\n    printf '%b' \"$UNMENTIONED\" | awk 'NF>0 {print \"- `\"$0\"`\"}'\n    printf '\\n## Recommended action\\n\\n'\n    printf 'Per L-S67-5: agent must run `git status --short` BEFORE authoring checkpoint\\n'\n    printf '\"what survived\" narrative. The hook found entries above that exist in working tree\\n'\n    printf 'but do NOT appear by basename in the checkpoint text.\\n\\n'\n    printf '1. Re-read git status; cross-check intentional vs missed\\n'\n    printf '2. If intentional omission → ignore (false positive — consider extending whitelist via\\n'\n    printf '   `STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST` env var)\\n'\n    printf '3. If missed → amend checkpoint to reflect actual state (M-S67-3 recurrence pattern)\\n'\n  } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n  printf 'WARN: %d unmentioned git-status entry(ies) in checkpoint — see %s\\n' \\\n    \"$UNMENTIONED_COUNT\" \"$NOTIF_FILE\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"33731d95-873b-4a10-92f1-71cea071e6ad","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"c9bd3831-b7df-41f7-be20-c5e77bdf7468","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

fixtures via printf/cat heredoc.\n# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../pre-checkpoint-close-verifier.sh\"\nif [ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: hook not found at $HOOK\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nPASS=0\nFAIL=0\n\nTEMPDIR=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/proj\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nCKPT=\"$MEMORY_DIR/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nSESSION_READY=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-ready\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n\nsetup_sandbox() {\n  rm -rf \"$PROJECT_DIR\"\n  mkdir -p \"$MEMORY_DIR/checkpoints\" \"$NOTIF_DIR\"\n  ( cd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" && git init -q . && git config user.email t@t.t && git config user.name t ) >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nwrite_session_ready() {\n  printf 'ready_at=2026-05-06T00:00:00Z\\nsession_id=test-sess\\nsource=startup\\n' > \"$SESSION_READY\"\n  # Set mtime to a known older time so we can author checkpoint after it\n  touch -t 202605060000 \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || true\n}\n\nwrite_checkpoint() {\n  local content=\"$1\"\n  printf '%s' \"$content\" > \"$CKPT\"\n  # Touch newer (current time)\n  touch \"$CKPT\"\n}\n\ncount_notif() {\n  ls \"$NOTIF_DIR\"/*-checkpoint-mentions-incomplete.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l\n}\n\nassert_pass() { PASS=$((PASS+1)); printf 'PASS TC%s: %s\\n' \"$2\" \"$1\"; }\nassert_fail() { FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); printf 'FAIL TC%s: %s\\n' \"$2\" \"$1\"; }\n\nPAYLOAD_STOP='{\"hook_event_name\":\"Stop\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\"}'\nPAYLOAD_OTHER='{\"hook_event_name\":\"PreToolUse\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\"}'\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC1 — checkpoint mentions all git-status basenames → no notif\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nwrite_session_ready\necho \"untracked content\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/foo_module.py\"\nwrite_checkpoint \"checkpoint mentions foo_module.py explicitly here\"\n( cd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" && CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$PAYLOAD_STOP\" 2>/dev/null )\nN=\"$(count_notif)\"\nN=\"${N:-0}\"\nif [ \"$N\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"all git entries mentioned → no notification\" \"1\"\nelse\n  assert_fail \"TC1: notif=$N (expected 0)\" \"1\"\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC2 — checkpoint omits a git-status entry → notification + WARN\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nwrite_session_ready\necho \"p1\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/mentioned_file.py\"\necho \"p2\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/unmentioned_file.py\"\nwrite_checkpoint \"checkpoint mentions only mentioned_file.py here\"\nSTDERR=\"$(CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$PAYLOAD_STOP\" 2>&1 >/dev/null || true)\"\nN=\"$(count_notif)\"\nN=\"${N:-0}\"\nWARN_HIT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$STDERR\" | grep -c 'WARN.*unmentioned' 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nWARN_HIT=\"${WARN_HIT:-0}\"\nif [ \"$N\" -eq 1 ] && [ \"$WARN_HIT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"kill switch → no notification despite omission\" \"3\"\nelse\n  assert_fail \"TC3: notif=$N (expected 0)\" \"3\"\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC4 — non-Stop event (PreToolUse) → no scan\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nwrite_session_ready\necho \"p1\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/missing.py\"\nwrite_checkpoint \"checkpoint says nothing about that file\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$PAYLOAD_OTHER\" 2>/dev/null\nN=\"$(count_notif)\"\nN=\"${N:-0}\"\nif [ \"$N\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"PreToolUse event → no scan\" \"4\"\nelse\n  assert_fail \"TC4: notif=$N (expected 0)\" \"4\"\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC5 — checkpoint NOT modified this session (mtime <= session-ready) → skip\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\necho \"p1\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/missing.py\"\nwrite_checkpoint \"checkpoint says nothing\"\n# Make session-ready NEWER than checkpoint by writing it after\nsleep 1\nprintf 'ready_at=2026-05-06T01:00:00Z\\nsession_id=test-sess\\nsource=startup\\n' > \"$SESSION_READY\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$PAYLOAD_STOP\" 2>/dev/null\nN=\"$(count_notif)\"\nN=\"${N:-0}\"\nif [ \"$N\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"checkpoint mtime ≤ session-ready → skip (not authored this session)\" \"5\"\nelse\n  assert_fail \"TC5: notif=$N (expected 0)\" \"5\"\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC6 — whitelist auto-generated paths skipped\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nwrite_session_ready\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/indexes\" \\\n         \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/learning-data/index\" \\\n         \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/raw-sessions\" \\\n         \"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\necho \"x\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/indexes/lesson-registry.tsv\"\necho \"x\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/learning-data/index/manifest.json\"\necho \"x\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/raw-sessions/2026-05-06-session-X.md\"\necho \"x\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.last-event-ts-foo\"\necho \"x\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/component-telemetry.jsonl\"\necho \"x\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.attestation-checked-something\"\necho \"x\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications/some-notif.md\"\nwrite_checkpoint \"checkpoint mentions nothing about these auto-files\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$PAYLOAD_STOP\" 2>/dev/null\nN=\"$(count_notif)\"\nN=\"${N:-0}\"\n# The newly-written own notif from THIS hook would land in NOTIF_DIR but only if the\n# hook actually fired. Final notif count should be 0.\nWHITELISTED_NOTIFS=\"$(ls \"$NOTIF_DIR\"/*-checkpoint-mentions-incomplete.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l || true)\"\nWHITELISTED_NOTIFS=\"${WHITELISTED_NOTIFS:-0}\"\nif [ \"$WHITELISTED_NOTIFS\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"whitelist auto-paths (indexes/learning-data/raw-sessions/notifications) → no notification\" \"6\"\nelse\n  assert_fail \"TC6: whitelisted_notifs=$WHITELISTED_NOTIFS (expected 0)\" \"6\"\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC7 — extra whitelist via env var\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nwrite_session_ready\necho \"p\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/custom_skip.txt\"\necho \"p\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/should_flag.py\"\nwrite_checkpoint \"checkpoint mentions only should_flag.py\"\nSTOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST='custom_skip\\.txt' \\\n  CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$PAYLOAD_STOP\" 2>/dev/null\nN=\"$(count_notif)\"\nN=\"${N:-0}\"\nif [ \"$N\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"extra whitelist via env → custom path skipped\" \"7\"\nelse\n  # If a notif WAS generated, check it doesn't include custom_skip.txt\n  CONTENT=\"$(cat \"$NOTIF_DIR\"/*-checkpoint-mentions-incomplete.md 2>/dev/null)\"\n  if printf '%s' \"$CONTENT\" | grep -qF \"custom_skip.txt\"; then\n    assert_fail \"TC7: extra whitelist not honored (custom_skip.txt still in notif)\" \"7\"\n  else\n    assert_fail \"TC7: notif=$N (expected 0)\" \"7\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC8 — clean working tree → no notif (graceful early exit)\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nwrite_session_ready\nwrite_checkpoint \"checkpoint authored but no working-tree changes\"\nCLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$PAYLOAD_STOP\" 2>/dev/null\nEC=$?\nN=\"$(count_notif)\"\nN=\"${N:-0}\"\nif [ \"$EC\" -eq 0 ] && [ \"$N\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"clean working tree → exit 0, no notification\" \"8\"\nelse\n  assert_fail \"TC8: exit=$EC notif=$N (expected 0/0)\" \"8\"\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Summary\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nTOTAL=$((PASS+FAIL))\necho \"\"\necho \"=== Results: PASS=$PASS FAIL=$FAIL ($TOTAL TCs) ===\"\nif [ \"$FAIL\" -eq 0 ]; bash\n# pre-checkpoint-close-verifier.sh — Stop hook detecting M-S67-3 pattern.\n# L-S67-5 mitigation: agent should run `git status --short` BEFORE authoring \"what\n# survived\" narrative in checkpoint close. M-S67-3 was: S67 checkpoint claimed only\n# D1 survived, but git status showed D2+D3+D5 also in working tree → narrative drift\n# from empirical state. This Stop hook detects post-hoc: if checkpoints/latest.md was\n# modified this session AND git status shows entries whose basenames are NOT mentioned\n# in the checkpoint text → emit WARN + notification (after whitelist filter).\n#\n# Wired: Stop (after autonomous-stop-watchdog.sh, before bash-hook-lint.sh)\n# Kill switch: STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_DISABLE=1\n# Detection signal: checkpoint mtime > .session-ready mtime (session-start-bootstrap.sh\n# writes .session-ready on every SessionStart per its line ~124).\n# Per L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware: if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y\n# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nCKPT=\"$MEMORY_DIR/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nSESSION_READY=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-ready\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n\n# Kill switch\nif [ \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_DISABLE:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Only fire on Stop event\nPAYLOAD=\"$(cat 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nHOOK_EVENT=\"\"\nif [ -n \"$PAYLOAD\" ]; then\n  HOOK_EVENT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"hook_event_name\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"hook_event_name\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\nfi\nif [ \"$HOOK_EVENT\" != \"Stop\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Skip if no checkpoint or no session marker\nif [ ! -f \"$CKPT\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$SESSION_READY\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Compare mtimes (Linux stat -c%Y / macOS stat -f%m fallback)\nCKPT_MTIME=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\nSESSION_START_TS=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n\n# If checkpoint not modified this session, skip\nif [ \"$CKPT_MTIME\" -le \"$SESSION_START_TS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Run git status --short in PROJECT_DIR\ncd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || exit 0\nGIT_STATUS=\"$(git status --short 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nif [ -z \"$GIT_STATUS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Whitelist: auto-generated/transient paths NOT expected in checkpoint narrative.\n# Tested against working-tree paths from `git status --short` output (after status-code prefix strip).\n# Configurable via env STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST (extra ERE patterns appended).\nWHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/sessions-rollup\\.tsv|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/)'\nif [ -n \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST:-}\" ]; then\n  WHITELIST_PATTERN=\"${WHITELIST_PATTERN}|${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST}\"\nfi\n\nUNMENTIONED=\"\"\nUNMENTIONED_COUNT=0\n\n# Iterate git status entries; skip whitelisted; check basename in checkpoint\nwhile IFS= read -r line; do\n  if [ -z \"$line\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  # `git status --short` output: 2-char status code + space + path (or rename has \" -> \")\n  # Strip first 3 chars to get path\n  path=\"${line:3}\"\n  # Handle rename: \"old -> new\" — take new path\n  if printf '%s' \"$path\" | grep -q ' -> '; then\n    path=\"$(printf '%s' \"$path\" | sed 's/.* -> //')\"\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"$path\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  # Whitelist check\n  if printf '%s' \"$path\" | grep -qE \"$WHITELIST_PATTERN\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  bn=\"$(basename \"$path\")\"\n  # Look for basename literal in checkpoint text\n  if ! grep -qF \"$bn\" \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    UNMENTIONED_COUNT=$((UNMENTIONED_COUNT + 1))\n    UNMENTIONED=\"${UNMENTIONED}${path}\\n\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$GIT_STATUS\"\n\nif [ \"$UNMENTIONED_COUNT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  TS=\"$(date -u +%FT%TZ 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  mkdir -p \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/${TS//[:.]/-}-checkpoint-mentions-incomplete.md\"\n  {\n    printf '# Checkpoint mentions incomplete (L-S67-5 / M-S67-3 prevention)\\n\\n'\n    printf '**Detected at**: %s (Stop hook)\\n' \"$TS\"\n    printf '**Checkpoint**: `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md`\\n'\n    printf '**Checkpoint mtime > session start**: %s > %s\\n\\n' \"$CKPT_MTIME\" \"$SESSION_START_TS\"\n    printf '## Git-status entries NOT mentioned by basename in checkpoint (after whitelist filter)\\n\\n'\n    printf '%b' \"$UNMENTIONED\" | awk 'NF>0 {print \"- `\"$0\"`\"}'\n    printf '\\n## Recommended action\\n\\n'\n    printf 'Per L-S67-5: agent must run `git status --short` BEFORE authoring checkpoint\\n'\n    printf '\"what survived\" narrative. The hook found entries above that exist in working tree\\n'\n    printf 'but do NOT appear by basename in the checkpoint text.\\n\\n'\n    printf '1. Re-read git status; cross-check intentional vs missed\\n'\n    printf '2. If intentional omission → ignore (false positive — consider extending whitelist via\\n'\n    printf '   `STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST` env var)\\n'\n    printf '3. If missed → amend checkpoint to reflect actual state (M-S67-3 recurrence pattern)\\n'\n  } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n  printf 'WARN: %d unmentioned git-status entry(ies) in checkpoint — see %s\\n' \\\n    \"$UNMENTIONED_COUNT\" \"$NOTIF_FILE\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":65,"oldLines":7,"newStart":65,"newLines":7,"lines":[" # Whitelist: auto-generated/transient paths NOT expected in checkpoint narrative."," # Tested against working-tree paths from `git status --short` output (after status-code prefix strip)."," # Configurable via env STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST (extra ERE patterns appended).","-WHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/sessions-rollup\\.tsv|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/)'","+WHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|checkpoints/latest\\.md|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/sessions-rollup\\.tsv|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/)'"," if [ -n \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST:-}\" ]; do\n    if [ -f \"$f\" ]; then\n      count=$((count+1))\n    fi\n  done\n  echo \"$count\"\n}","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# pre-checkpoint-close-verifier-fire-test.sh — Plan 012 D2 firing test.\n# Tests externally-observable behavior per L-S59-1:\n#   - notification file created on detection\n#   - WARN to stderr\n#   - whitelist filter (auto-generated paths skipped)\n#   - kill switch honored\n#   - hook event filter (only Stop)\n#   - mtime gate (checkpoint not modified this session → skip)\n# Per L-S52-3 success-path: real-data smoke + ≥6 TCs (≥1 positive + ≥3 negative + kill-switch).\n# Per L-S62-1: no yes|head patterns; fixtures via printf/cat heredoc.\n# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only.\nset -euo pipefail\n\nSCRIPT_DIR=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")\" && pwd)\"\nHOOK=\"$SCRIPT_DIR/../pre-checkpoint-close-verifier.sh\"\nif [ ! -f \"$HOOK\" ]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: hook not found at $HOOK\"\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nPASS=0\nFAIL=0\n\nTEMPDIR=\"$(mktemp -d)\"\ntrap 'rm -rf \"$TEMPDIR\"' EXIT\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"$TEMPDIR/proj\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nCKPT=\"$MEMORY_DIR/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nSESSION_READY=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-ready\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n\nsetup_sandbox() {\n  rm -rf \"$PROJECT_DIR\"\n  mkdir -p \"$MEMORY_DIR/checkpoints\" \"$NOTIF_DIR\"\n  ( cd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" && git init -q . && git config user.email t@t.t && git config user.name t ) >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n}\n\nwrite_session_ready() {\n  printf 'ready_at=2026-05-06T00:00:00Z\\nsession_id=test-sess\\nsource=startup\\n' > \"$SESSION_READY\"\n  # Set mtime to a known older time so we can author checkpoint after it\n  touch -t 202605060000 \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || true\n}\n\nwrite_checkpoint() {\n  local content=\"$1\"\n  printf '%s' \"$content\" > \"$CKPT\"\n  # Touch newer (current time)\n  touch \"$CKPT\"\n}\n\ncount_notif() {\n  ls \"$NOTIF_DIR\"/*-checkpoint-mentions-incomplete.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l\n}\n\nassert_pass() { PASS=$((PASS+1)); printf 'PASS TC%s: %s\\n' \"$2\" \"$1\"; }\nassert_fail() { FAIL=$((FAIL+1)); printf 'FAIL TC%s: %s\\n' \"$2\" \"$1\"; }\n\nPAYLOAD_STOP='{\"hook_event_name\":\"Stop\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\"}'\nPAYLOAD_OTHER='{\"hook_event_name\":\"PreToolUse\",\"session_id\":\"test-sess\"}'\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC1 — checkpoint mentions all git-status basenames → no notif\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nwrite_session_ready\necho \"untracked content\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/foo_module.py\"\nwrite_checkpoint \"checkpoint mentions foo_module.py explicitly here\"\n( cd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" && CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$PAYLOAD_STOP\" 2>/dev/null )\nN=\"$(count_notif)\"\nN=\"${N:-0}\"\nif [ \"$N\" -eq 0 ]; then\n  assert_pass \"all git entries mentioned → no notification\" \"1\"\nelse\n  assert_fail \"TC1: notif=$N (expected 0)\" \"1\"\nfi\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\n# TC2 — checkpoint omits a git-status entry → notification + WARN\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------\nsetup_sandbox\nwrite_session_ready\necho \"p1\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/mentioned_file.py\"\necho \"p2\" > \"$PROJECT_DIR/unmentioned_file.py\"\nwrite_checkpoint \"checkpoint mentions only mentioned_file.py here\"\nSTDERR=\"$(CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR\" bash \"$HOOK\" <<< \"$PAYLOAD_STOP\" 2>&1 >/dev/null || true)\"\nN=\"$(count_notif)\"\nN=\"${N:-0}\"\nWARN_HIT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$STDERR\" | grep -c 'WARN.*unmentioned' 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nWARN_HIT=\"${WARN_HIT:-0}\"\nif [ \"$N\" -eq 1 ] && [ \"$WARN_HIT\" -ge 1 ]; {"parentUuid":"1ef51276-2185-4d4d-9ec3-2cddee885c4b","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"ab04ef1e-64a3-48b8-ad97-2385041787d1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016rborok3pykhjtg7r42qCf","type":"tool_result","content":"1620\t---\n1621\t\n1622\t### L-S68-2: `find` on non-existent path under `set -uo pipefail` propagates non-zero through pipe → ERR trap → silent exit 0; ALWAYS guard with `[ -f ]` check\n1623\t\n1624\t**Date**: 2026-05-06 (caught at D6 firing-test stage per L-S52-3 success-path)\n1625\t**Origin**: D6 dogfood test exposed pre-existing bug in `lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh`. Hook ran `find \"$SA_DIR/known-issues.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]'`. When file doesn't exist, `find` exits non-zero. With `set -uo pipefail`, the pipe inherits non-zero. With `trap 'exit 0' ERR`, ERR trap fires → hook silent-exits 0 BEFORE reaching dormancy detection branch. In production this was masked because target files always exist; in test sandbox without those files the bug surfaced.\n1626\t\n1627\t**Why**: Common bash pattern `find PATH ... | wc -l` is unsafe under pipefail+ERR-trap when PATH may not exist. The `2>/dev/null` redirect only swallows stderr — it does NOT change find's exit code.\n1628\t\n1629\t**How to apply**:\n1630\t- BEFORE `find $PATH ...` in any pipeline under `set -uo pipefail` + ERR-trap, always guard:\n1631\t  ```bash\n1632\t  COUNT=0\n1633\t  if [ -f \"$PATH\" ]; then  # or [ -d ] or [ -e ]\n1634\t    COUNT=$(find \"$PATH\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n1635\t  fi\n1636\t  ```\n1637\t- Alternative: wrap pipe in `{ find ... 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l` (less obvious; prefer `[ -f ]` guard).\n1638\t- DO NOT rely on `2>/dev/null` to prevent ERR trap — it only redirects stderr, not exit code.\n1639\t\n1640\t**Severity**: MEDIUM (silent fail mode — hook does nothing without error signal; in production rare because target files usually exist; in test sandbox routine)\n1641\t**Auto-detect**: PARTIAL — bash-hook-lint could grep `find ... 2>/dev/null |` patterns and require preceding `[ -f ]` guard. Defer to next promote-rule cycle.\n1642\t\n1643\t**Anti-example** (the bug):\n1644\t```bash\n1645\tKI_RECENT=$(find \"$SA_DIR/known-issues.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n1646\t# If file missing: find exit 1 → pipefail → ERR trap → exit 0 (silent fail)\n1647\t```\n1648\t\n1649\t**Correct example** (the fix):\n1650\t```bash\n1651\tKI_RECENT=0\n1652\tif [ -f \"$SA_DIR/known-issues.md\" ]; then\n1653\t  KI_RECENT=$(find \"$SA_DIR/known-issues.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n1654\tfi\n1655\t```\n1656\t\n1657\t**Where tracked**:\n1658\t- This entry (L-S68-2)\n1659\t- `scripts/hooks/lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh:38-50` (the fix applies the rule)\n1660\t- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/etl-queue-producer-fire-test.sh` (TC1+TC2 verify the path doesn't silent-fail)\n1661\t- Companion to L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware doctrine (uses if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y); L-S68-2 extends to FIND-pipefail edge case\n1662\t\n1663\t**Dogfood validation per L-S67-2 binding**: D6 firing-test caught this bug in own deploy → fixed in same session → all 7/7 TCs PASS post-fix → L-S52-3 success-path doctrine validated again.\n1664\t"}]},"uuid":"9dd34b9c-f883-44a0-b7c9-2f0a0aaef5ec","timestamp":"2026-05-06T04:01:00.970Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","content":"---\n\n### L-S68-2: `find` on non-existent path under `set -uo pipefail` propagates non-zero through pipe → ERR trap → silent exit 0; ALWAYS guard with `[ -f ]` check\n\n**Date**: 2026-05-06 (caught at D6 firing-test stage per L-S52-3 success-path)\n**Origin**: D6 dogfood test exposed pre-existing bug in `lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh`. Hook ran `find \"$SA_DIR/known-issues.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]'`. When file doesn't exist, `find` exits non-zero. With `set -uo pipefail`, the pipe inherits non-zero. With `trap 'exit 0' ERR`, ERR trap fires → hook silent-exits 0 BEFORE reaching dormancy detection branch. In production this was masked because target files always exist; in test sandbox without those files the bug surfaced.\n\n**Why**: Common bash pattern `find PATH ... | wc -l` is unsafe under pipefail+ERR-trap when PATH may not exist. The `2>/dev/null` redirect only swallows stderr — it does NOT change find's exit code.\n\n**How to apply**:\n- BEFORE `find $PATH ...` in any pipeline under `set -uo pipefail` + ERR-trap, always guard:\n  ```bash\n  COUNT=0\n  if [ -f \"$PATH\" ]; then  # or [ -d ] or [ -e ]\n    COUNT=$(find \"$PATH\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n  fi\n  ```\n- Alternative: wrap pipe in `{ find ... 2>/dev/null || true; } | wc -l` (less obvious; prefer `[ -f ]` guard).\n- DO NOT rely on `2>/dev/null` to prevent ERR trap — it only redirects stderr, not exit code.\n\n**Severity**: MEDIUM (silent fail mode — hook does nothing without error signal; in production rare because target files usually exist; in test sandbox routine)\n**Auto-detect**: PARTIAL — bash-hook-lint could grep `find ... 2>/dev/null |` patterns and require preceding `[ -f ]` guard. Defer to next promote-rule cycle.\n\n**Anti-example** (the bug):\n```bash\nKI_RECENT=$(find \"$SA_DIR/known-issues.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\n# If file missing: find exit 1 → pipefail → ERR trap → exit 0 (silent fail)\n```\n\n**Correct example** (the fix):\n```bash\nKI_RECENT=0\nif [ -f \"$SA_DIR/known-issues.md\" ]; then\n  KI_RECENT=$(find \"$SA_DIR/known-issues.md\" -mtime -1 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d '[:space:]')\nfi\n```\n\n**Where tracked**:\n- This entry (L-S68-2)\n- `scripts/hooks/lesson-synthesis-watchdog.sh:38-50` (the fix applies the rule)\n- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/etl-queue-producer-fire-test.sh` (TC1+TC2 verify the path doesn't silent-fail)\n- Companion to L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware doctrine (uses if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y); L-S68-2 extends to FIND-pipefail edge case\n\n**Dogfood validation per L-S67-2 binding**: D6 firing-test caught this bug in own deploy → fixed in same session → all 7/7 TCs PASS post-fix → L-S52-3 success-path doctrine validated again.\n","numLines":45,"startLine":1620,"totalLines":1664}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1ef51276-2185-4d4d-9ec3-2cddee885c4b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"c9bd3831-b7df-41f7-be20-c5e77bdf7468","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"3f0b6a97-bf75-4228-8a7d-a032d88c0474","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"ab04ef1e-64a3-48b8-ad97-2385041787d1","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Hj6NGQJYMiiZdjgr6vwGUJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1660\t- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/etl-queue-producer-fire-test.sh` (TC1+TC2 verify the path doesn't silent-fail)\n1661\t- Companion to L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware doctrine (uses if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y); L-S68-2 extends to FIND-pipefail edge case\n1662\t\n1663\t**Dogfood validation per L-S67-2 binding**: D6 firing-test caught this bug in own deploy → fixed in same session → all 7/7 TCs PASS post-fix → L-S52-3 success-path doctrine validated again.\n1664\t"}]},"uuid":"efd69575-6219-4beb-8cd5-3f0b5fa6e5a5","timestamp":"2026-05-06T04:01:31.041Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","content":"- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/etl-queue-producer-fire-test.sh` (TC1+TC2 verify the path doesn't silent-fail)\n- Companion to L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware doctrine (uses if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y); L-S68-2 extends to FIND-pipefail edge case\n\n**Dogfood validation per L-S67-2 binding**: D6 firing-test caught this bug in own deploy → fixed in same session → all 7/7 TCs PASS post-fix → L-S52-3 success-path doctrine validated again.\n","numLines":5,"startLine":1660,"totalLines":1664}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3f0b6a97-bf75-4228-8a7d-a032d88c0474","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"c9bd3831-b7df-41f7-be20-c5e77bdf7468","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"efd69575-6219-4beb-8cd5-3f0b5fa6e5a5","isSidechain":false,"message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-7","id":"msg_01RZDoWEytzuHm6YnpMYGHFq","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011t3tz4LmLQLmpMPskQvCu7","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\agent-workspace\\memory\\agent-notes.md","old_string":"**Dogfood validation per L-S67-2 binding**: D6 firing-test caught this bug in own deploy → fixed in same session → all 7/7 TCs PASS post-fix → L-S52-3 success-path doctrine validated again.\n","new_string":"**Dogfood validation per L-S67-2 binding**: D6 firing-test caught this bug in own deploy → fixed in same session → all 7/7 TCs PASS post-fix → L-S52-3 success-path doctrine validated again.\n\n---\n\n### L-S69-1: Hook self-reference rule — artifact-verifier hooks must whitelist their own verification target\n\n**Date**: 2026-05-06 (caught at D2 firing-test stage during Plan 012 deploy)\n**Origin**: `pre-checkpoint-close-verifier.sh` (Plan 012 D2) initially flagged `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md` itself as \"unmentioned in checkpoint\" — the verifier reading checkpoint complained that its own target file wasn't mentioned by basename. Caught in TC1 of firing-test (single iteration-fix); whitelist extended.\n\n**Why**: When a Stop-hook scans git-status entries against the BODY of an artifact and emits warnings for unmentioned entries, the artifact's own filename is by definition present in git-status (it was just modified) but logically need not appear in its own body. Self-reference is a tautology, not a signal. Without the whitelist, the hook generates false-positive on every run where the artifact itself was modified.\n\n**How to apply** (general rule for artifact-verifier hooks):\n- ANY Stop hook that reads file X's content and verifies properties of git-status entries → whitelist file X's path itself\n- Document the rationale in the hook's WHITELIST_PATTERN comment so future maintainers don't re-introduce the false-positive\n- Prefer explicit path whitelist over heuristics (e.g. don't use \"skip files modified at same mtime as target\")\n\n**Severity**: LOW (false-positive noise; not silent-fail)\n**Auto-detect**: PARTIAL — bash-hook-lint could flag hooks that read file X AND grep file X-derived basename without whitelisting X. Defer to next promote-rule cycle.\n\n**Anti-example** (initial D2 hook before fix):\n```bash\n# WHITELIST_PATTERN omitting checkpoints/latest.md\n# → git status sees checkpoints/latest.md as M\n# → hook complains \"latest.md unmentioned in latest.md\" (tautology)\n```\n\n**Correct example** (D2 hook post-fix, line 64):\n```bash\nWHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(...|checkpoints/latest\\.md|...)|...)'\n```\n\n**Where tracked**:\n- This entry (L-S69-1)\n- `scripts/hooks/pre-checkpoint-close-verifier.sh:64` (whitelist pattern includes checkpoints/latest.md)\n- `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/pre-checkpoint-close-verifier-fire-test.sh:TC1` (regression test would catch re-introduction)\n\n**Dogfood validation**: Plan 012 D2 firing-test 8/8 PASS; production dogfood early-exits (correct: S69 hasn't authored new checkpoint yet).\n\n---\n\n### L-S69-2: Hook deployment validates pre-existing lesson — retroactive backfill needed for legacy artifacts\n\n**Date**: 2026-05-06 (Plan 012 D1 dogfood findings)\n**Origin**: After deploying `session-start-scan-unattested-observations.sh` (L-S68-1 mitigation), production dogfood detected 1 LEGACY unattested observation: `sandwich-dev-S66-BC-7-track-J.md`. This file pre-dates D1 attestation hook deployment (D1 deployed S67 per Plan 011). The scan correctly identified it as \"lacking BOTH marker AND log row\" — but the gap is HISTORICAL, not a M-S67-3 / M-S66-1 recurrence.\n\n**Why**: When a deterministic detection hook is newly deployed, its first run on production state SURFACES the entire historical backlog. Without a retroactive backfill policy, the registry accumulates noise; agents reading boot-summary cannot distinguish historical-already-resolved gaps from fresh-and-actionable gaps.\n\n**How to apply** (retroactive backfill policy):\n1. After deploying ANY new audit/scan hook, run dogfood against production immediately\n2. Triage each detected legacy entry against post-mortem records:\n   - If the underlying issue was already addressed in a prior session (mistake-log / session log) → manually `touch` the marker (or append a log row with `verdict: LEGACY-RESOLVED`) to clear the registry\n   - If still genuinely open → file as new issue\n3. After triage, re-run dogfood; registry should be empty (steady state) so future detections are signal not noise\n\n**Severity**: LOW (procedural; affects signal-to-noise of new hooks; not silent-fail)\n**Auto-detect**: NO — this is a deployment-time procedure, not a code rule.\n\n**Anti-example**:\n```\nDeploy new hook → don't dogfood → registry accumulates legacy noise → agent ignores future alerts as \"always-on backlog noise\"\n```\n\n**Correct example** (S69 application):\n```\nDeploy session-start-scan-unattested-observations.sh\n→ dogfood: 1 legacy entry (sandwich-dev-S66-BC-7-track-J)\n→ triage: M-S66-1 cataloged + dev's S52 work was reverted; obs is informational artifact, not actionable\n→ defer retroactive marker decision to next harness burst (not auto-clearing yet — S69 footprint kept lean)\n```\n\n**Where tracked**:\n- This entry (L-S69-2)\n- `scripts/hooks/session-start-scan-unattested-observations.sh` (Plan 012 D1)\n- `agent-workspace/memory/.unattested-observations.tsv` (registry; 1 row at S69 close: S66-BC-7-track-J pending triage policy)\n\n**Companion to L-S67-2**: eat-own-dogfood at deploy time mandatory (this lesson extends — dogfood OUTPUT must be triaged, not just observed for hook function).\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1966,"cache_read_input_tokens":178936,"output_tokens":2312,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1966,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1,"output_tokens":2312,"cache_read_input_tokens":178936,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1966,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1966},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"}},"requestId":"req_011CakeedsosETHufYtjELTy","type":"assistant","uuid":"16e5a631-94e2-442d-b495-f51b5117df94","timestamp":"2026-05-06T04:02:08.256Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"c9bd3831-b7df-41f7-be20-c5e77bdf7468","version":"2.1.124","gitBranch":"main"}

successfully."}]},"uuid":"03671bad-5cf4-473b-9e31-988cc6a01bb4","timestamp":"2026-05-06T04:05:43.958Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\pre-checkpoint-close-verifier.sh","oldString":"WHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|checkpoints/latest\\.md|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/sessions-rollup\\.tsv|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/)'","newString":"WHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|checkpoints/latest\\.md|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/(sessions-rollup\\.tsv|profiles/)|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md|boot-summary\\.md|attestation-log\\.tsv)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/|.*\\.tmp$)'","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# pre-checkpoint-close-verifier.sh — Stop hook detecting M-S67-3 pattern.\n# L-S67-5 mitigation: agent should run `git status --short` BEFORE authoring \"what\n# survived\" narrative in checkpoint close. M-S67-3 was: S67 checkpoint claimed only\n# D1 survived, but git status showed D2+D3+D5 also in working tree → narrative drift\n# from empirical state. This Stop hook detects post-hoc: if checkpoints/latest.md was\n# modified this session AND git status shows entries whose basenames are NOT mentioned\n# in the checkpoint text → emit WARN + notification (after whitelist filter).\n#\n# Wired: Stop (after autonomous-stop-watchdog.sh, before bash-hook-lint.sh)\n# Kill switch: STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_DISABLE=1\n# Detection signal: checkpoint mtime > .session-ready mtime (session-start-bootstrap.sh\n# writes .session-ready on every SessionStart per its line ~124).\n# Per L-S58-1 ERR-trap-aware: if/then/fi not [ x ] && Y\n# Per L-S11-1 portability: bash + awk + grep + sed only\n\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nMEMORY_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nCKPT=\"$MEMORY_DIR/checkpoints/latest.md\"\nSESSION_READY=\"$MEMORY_DIR/.session-ready\"\nNOTIF_DIR=\"$PROJECT_DIR/human-workspace/notifications\"\n\n# Kill switch\nif [ \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_DISABLE:-0}\" = \"1\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Only fire on Stop event\nPAYLOAD=\"$(cat 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nHOOK_EVENT=\"\"\nif [ -n \"$PAYLOAD\" ]; then\n  HOOK_EVENT=\"$(printf '%s' \"$PAYLOAD\" | grep -o '\"hook_event_name\":\"[^\"]*\"' 2>/dev/null | head -1 | sed 's/\"hook_event_name\":\"//;s/\"//' || true)\"\nfi\nif [ \"$HOOK_EVENT\" != \"Stop\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Skip if no checkpoint or no session marker\nif [ ! -f \"$CKPT\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\nif [ ! -f \"$SESSION_READY\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Compare mtimes (Linux stat -c%Y / macOS stat -f%m fallback)\nCKPT_MTIME=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\nSESSION_START_TS=\"$(stat -c%Y \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f%m \"$SESSION_READY\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\"\n\n# If checkpoint not modified this session, skip\nif [ \"$CKPT_MTIME\" -le \"$SESSION_START_TS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Run git status --short in PROJECT_DIR\ncd \"$PROJECT_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || exit 0\nGIT_STATUS=\"$(git status --short 2>/dev/null || true)\"\nif [ -z \"$GIT_STATUS\" ]; then\n  exit 0\nfi\n\n# Whitelist: auto-generated/transient paths NOT expected in checkpoint narrative.\n# Tested against working-tree paths from `git status --short` output (after status-code prefix strip).\n# Configurable via env STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST (extra ERE patterns appended).\nWHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|checkpoints/latest\\.md|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/sessions-rollup\\.tsv|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/)'\nif [ -n \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST:-}\" ]; then\n  WHITELIST_PATTERN=\"${WHITELIST_PATTERN}|${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST}\"\nfi\n\nUNMENTIONED=\"\"\nUNMENTIONED_COUNT=0\n\n# Iterate git status entries; skip whitelisted; check basename in checkpoint\nwhile IFS= read -r line; do\n  if [ -z \"$line\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  # `git status --short` output: 2-char status code + space + path (or rename has \" -> \")\n  # Strip first 3 chars to get path\n  path=\"${line:3}\"\n  # Handle rename: \"old -> new\" — take new path\n  if printf '%s' \"$path\" | grep -q ' -> '; then\n    path=\"$(printf '%s' \"$path\" | sed 's/.* -> //')\"\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"$path\" ]; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  # Whitelist check\n  if printf '%s' \"$path\" | grep -qE \"$WHITELIST_PATTERN\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    continue\n  fi\n  bn=\"$(basename \"$path\")\"\n  # Look for basename literal in checkpoint text\n  if ! grep -qF \"$bn\" \"$CKPT\" 2>/dev/null; then\n    UNMENTIONED_COUNT=$((UNMENTIONED_COUNT + 1))\n    UNMENTIONED=\"${UNMENTIONED}${path}\\n\"\n  fi\ndone <<< \"$GIT_STATUS\"\n\nif [ \"$UNMENTIONED_COUNT\" -ge 1 ]; then\n  TS=\"$(date -u +%FT%TZ 2>/dev/null || true)\"\n  mkdir -p \"$NOTIF_DIR\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  NOTIF_FILE=\"$NOTIF_DIR/${TS//[:.]/-}-checkpoint-mentions-incomplete.md\"\n  {\n    printf '# Checkpoint mentions incomplete (L-S67-5 / M-S67-3 prevention)\\n\\n'\n    printf '**Detected at**: %s (Stop hook)\\n' \"$TS\"\n    printf '**Checkpoint**: `agent-workspace/memory/checkpoints/latest.md`\\n'\n    printf '**Checkpoint mtime > session start**: %s > %s\\n\\n' \"$CKPT_MTIME\" \"$SESSION_START_TS\"\n    printf '## Git-status entries NOT mentioned by basename in checkpoint (after whitelist filter)\\n\\n'\n    printf '%b' \"$UNMENTIONED\" | awk 'NF>0 {print \"- `\"$0\"`\"}'\n    printf '\\n## Recommended action\\n\\n'\n    printf 'Per L-S67-5: agent must run `git status --short` BEFORE authoring checkpoint\\n'\n    printf '\"what survived\" narrative. The hook found entries above that exist in working tree\\n'\n    printf 'but do NOT appear by basename in the checkpoint text.\\n\\n'\n    printf '1. Re-read git status; cross-check intentional vs missed\\n'\n    printf '2. If intentional omission → ignore (false positive — consider extending whitelist via\\n'\n    printf '   `STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST` env var)\\n'\n    printf '3. If missed → amend checkpoint to reflect actual state (M-S67-3 recurrence pattern)\\n'\n  } > \"$NOTIF_FILE\"\n  printf 'WARN: %d unmentioned git-status entry(ies) in checkpoint — see %s\\n' \\\n    \"$UNMENTIONED_COUNT\" \"$NOTIF_FILE\" >&2\nfi\n\nexit 0\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":65,"oldLines":7,"newStart":65,"newLines":7,"lines":[" # Whitelist: auto-generated/transient paths NOT expected in checkpoint narrative."," # Tested against working-tree paths from `git status --short` output (after status-code prefix strip)."," # Configurable via env STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST (extra ERE patterns appended).","-WHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|checkpoints/latest\\.md|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/sessions-rollup\\.tsv|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/)'","+WHITELIST_PATTERN='^(agent-workspace/memory/(indexes/|\\.|etl-queue/|\\.precompact-snapshots/|checkpoints/latest\\.md|component-telemetry\\.jsonl|cost-ledger\\.tsv|dispatch\\.jsonl|\\.continue-injector|\\.last-event-ts|\\.session-|\\.qa-last-scan|\\.transcript-tokens|\\.attestation-checked-|\\.dispatch-pending|\\.profile-template-fired|\\.mode-d-recovery-fired|\\.unattested-observations\\.tsv|sync-state\\.md|sync-tracker/_index\\.md|self-awareness/(sessions-rollup\\.tsv|profiles/)|drift-logs/[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}-rollup\\.md|boot-summary\\.md|attestation-log\\.tsv)|agent-workspace/learning-data/|agent-workspace/raw-sessions/|human-workspace/notifications/|.*\\.tmp$)'"," if [ -n \"${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST:-}\" ]; then","   WHITELIST_PATTERN=\"${WHITELIST_PATTERN}|${STOCKFORGE_CHECKPOINT_VERIFIER_EXTRA_WHITELIST}\"","
